Method: 提出FBCC框架，包含一个持续教师网络（含聚类投影头）和多个轻量级任务特定学生网络。通过前向-后向蒸馏双阶段过程：前向阶段让学生学习新任务聚类结构，后向阶段将学生知识蒸馏回教师，使教师在学习新聚类的同时保留先前发现的聚类结构，无需存储历史数据。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线方法，并显著降低了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC是首个针对无监督持续聚类（UCC）任务的开创性方法，验证了在不依赖标签和历史数据存储的条件下，通过双向蒸馏机制可有效实现持续聚类并缓解遗忘。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [133] [Graph Neural Network leveraging Higher-order Class Label Connectivity for Heterophilous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07475)
*Takuto Takahashi,Itsuki Nakayama,Takahiro Mitani,Ryosuke Kikuchi,Yuya Sasaki,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异配有向图中GNN无法捕获高阶类别标签连通性的问题，提出标签上下文分类器(LCC)，通过四种不同游走类型生成标签上下文嵌入，并可自适应地与任意GNN集成，实验表明该集成方法在异配有向图节点分类上优于SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在同配图上表现优异，但在异配图上性能有限，尤其是基于图卷积网络的GNN无法捕获异配图中常见的高阶类别标签连通性。

Method: 提出LCC，通过四种不同类型的游走生成标签上下文嵌入来估计目标节点的类别标签，并设计自适应机制将LCC与任意GNN进行集成，动态学习两者的重要性权重。

Result: 集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图节点分类任务上优于现有SOTA方法，标签上下文嵌入有效提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: LCC能够有效捕获高阶类别标签连通性，弥补了传统GNN在异配图上的不足，且具有即插即用的灵活性。

Abstract: Node classification in graph neural networks (GNNs) has been widely applied in various fields of graph analysis. GNNs achieve high-accuracy node classification in homophilous graphs, where nodes with the same class label tend to be connected. However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.

</details>


### [134] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件漂移（conditional drifting）的生成式代理模型，在VAE潜空间中进行标签感知掩码学习，实现单次高质量流场生成，速度比迭代扩散方法快两个数量级，并具备泛化到未知几何的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD虽然能提供高保真流场用于室内环境优化，但计算成本高，限制了快速探索。现有生成式代理模型虽然比确定性网络更好地建模分布，但迭代采样速度慢。因此需要一种高质量、单次生成的替代方案。

Method: 将新颖的生成式漂移框架（generative drifting）适配到流体力学领域，提出条件架构：在学习的VAE潜空间中执行漂移，并使用标签感知掩码（label-aware masking）使生成样本与其边界条件对齐。此外还提出空间条件变体以泛化到未知几何。

Result: 标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上与迭代扩散方法相当，但运行速度快两个数量级；空间条件变体展现出对未知几何泛化的前景。

Conclusion: 条件漂移作为扩散方法的高效替代方案，能够在推理速度至关重要的场景中解锁实时CFD代理模型。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [135] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CascadeNet，一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，无需指定扩散模型即可从级联数据中恢复潜在影响网络，并通过Neyman正交去偏实现$\sqrt{n}$一致性和渐近正态性，在模拟和COVID-19真实数据中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法通常假设特定的扩散模型，当模型设定错误时性能严重下降。亟需一种不依赖具体扩散机制的普适性方法。

Method: CascadeNet通过Jacbian矩阵刻画一步转移函数中的影响结构：先构建灵活的转移函数估计器，再利用Riesz表示子进行Neyman正交去偏，得到去偏后的雅可比矩阵，实现$\sqrt{n}$一致估计和渐近正态推断。

Result: 在九种常见数据生成过程的模拟中，CascadeNet的网络恢复准确率最高；在西班牙52省COVID-19传播的真实应用中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实 mobility 网络显著相关，而基线方法无显著对齐。

Conclusion: CascadeNet提供了一种无需预设扩散模型的网络恢复框架，兼具统计可推断性和实际有效性，为级联数据下的因果网络发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [136] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个持续元学习框架，用于实现个性化虚拟心脏神经代理，能够在持续整合新数据的同时识别数据来源，并有效防止灾难性遗忘。通过基于记忆缓冲区的持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型，该框架可推断数据标识及其关系。在合成心脏数据上的实验显示，该方法在模拟预测、计算可扩展性和抗遗忘能力方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于元学习的个性化心脏代理方法假设静态且多样的训练分布，需要已知任务标识。当新数据顺序到达时，需用全部历史数据重新训练以避免灾难性遗忘，这在临床场景中不可行。本文旨在解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出持续元学习框架，利用持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型（Bayesian GMM）管理记忆缓冲区，以推断随时间变化的数据标识和关系，实现无需任务标识的持续个性化代理学习。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上，该方法在模拟预测性能、计算可扩展性和抗灾难性遗忘方面均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的持续元学习框架能有效解决临床场景中顺序数据到达时的模型持续学习问题，为个性化虚拟心脏模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [137] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经网络的二阶路径核插值公式，在Domingos一阶公式基础上引入曲率加权项，并针对随机梯度下降及其带动量版本推导了噪声协方差耦合项和记忆因子修正，给出了预测浓度的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练数据如何影响神经网络预测是现代学习理论的核心问题。Domingos (2020) 提出了一阶插值公式，但仅刻画了梯度对齐效应，忽略了损失曲面曲率和随机优化噪声的影响，需要更高阶的理论刻画。

Method: 在确定性梯度下降基础上展开二阶泰勒近似，导出曲率加权插值项；针对SGD引入mini-batch梯度噪声协方差与预测曲率的耦合项；进一步扩展至带动量的SGD，引入记忆因子修正权重；最后给出终端预测的浓度不等式。

Result: 得到了二阶路径核插值公式：预测由一阶核项、曲率加权项和（对于SGD）噪声-曲率耦合项组成；动量SGD保留相似结构但权重被记忆因子修正；预测围绕期望二阶表示波动，波动尺度可由浓度估计刻画。

Conclusion: 这些结果细化了神经网络预测的路径核解释，揭示了曲率和随机优化噪声对预测的具体贡献机制。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


### [138] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MG-ADSGD算法，通过结合Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速gossip平均，实现了分散式随机强凸优化中同时加速的通信复杂度\(\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right)\)，首次同时达到\(\sqrtκ\)和\(1/\sqrt{1-β}\)的加速依赖，为目前最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分散式随机优化方法无法同时实现加速的条件数\(\sqrtκ\)依赖和网络谱间隙\(1/\sqrt{1-β}\)依赖，而确定性方法已能同时达到这两项加速，存在显著的随机方法性能差距。

Method: 将Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速gossip平均相结合，核心创新在于将gossip轮数与mini-batch大小耦合，使额外通信轮次同时提升共识精度和降低梯度方差。

Result: MG-ADSGD达到通信复杂度\(\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right)\)，同时实现\(\sqrtκ\)和\(1/\sqrt{1-β}\)的加速依赖，是目前已知最优的分散式随机强凸优化通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的机制设计，首次弥合了分散式随机优化与确定性优化之间的加速差距，为大规模网络学习提供了更高效的通信方案。

Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

</details>


### [139] [Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07500)
*Fatema Siddika,Md Anwar Hossen,Tanwi Mallick,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETA 提出了一种基于稀疏专家混合的自适应稀疏子空间分解方法，通过将知识分离为任务特定专家和共享专家，并引入自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化，解决了LLM持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性困境，在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法对参数进行统一处理，未能区分任务特定知识与共享能力，导致学习新能力时灾难性遗忘旧知识。

Method: 提出SETA框架，核心包括：(1) 将参数空间自适应分解为任务特定专家（隔离任务特定模式）和共享专家（捕获通用特征）的稀疏子空间；(2) 自适应弹性锚定保护共享知识；(3) 路由感知正则化在权重和路由两个层面联合保护共享知识；(4) 统一门控网络在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。

Result: 在多个领域特定基准上，SETA在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上达到或超越当前最优持续学习方法，尤其在早期任务知识的保留和后向迁移方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过专家级别的知识分离和双重正则化机制，SETA有效解决了可塑性-稳定性冲突，为LLM持续学习提供了一种任务无关的稀疏分解方案。

Abstract: Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Odd-parity perturbations of trace-quadratic $f(R,T)$ black holes with anisotropic matter: admissible branches, axial ringdown, and a coupled-PINN benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06517)
*Mushtaq Ahmad,M. Farasat Shamir,Adnan Malik,Ahdab K. Althukair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了trace-quadratic $f(R,T)=R+αT^2$引力中静态黑洞的奇宇称引力扰动，发现可允许的参数分支位于负$w_r$区域，轴向谱由单一规范不变主方程控制。对于锚定分支$(w_r,w_t)=(-0.2,0.15)$，基本轴向$\ell=2$准正规模与Schwarzschild差异约22%，但$α$依赖性在统计上不显著。能量条件、修正平衡律和有效外部质量分数等诊断均表明该模型应解读为有效各向异性应力而非微观物理流体，轴向环振的主要可观测印记来自物质支撑分支本身而非迹耦合的直接变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究trace-quadratic $f(R,T)$引力中静态黑洞的奇宇称扰动，以识别支持正则视界、渐近平坦性和双曲演化的参数空间区域，并探讨该理论在环振信号中的可观测印记。

Method: 通过未约化的轴向系统及其主符号分析参数空间；采用精确Chebyshev方法计算准正规模；构建耦合物理信息神经网络(PINN)用于对比分支的基准测试；对能量条件、修正平衡律等进行闭合性审计。

Result: 可允许分支位于负$w_r$；轴向谱等价于含冻结有效各向异性流体的Einstein引力；$\ell=2$基模与Schwarzschild相差约22%，$α$依赖在$0\le α/M^2\le 0.3$范围内统计不显著；正$w_r$分支不满足背景正则性测试；能量条件诊断组合为正，有效外部质量分数约20%。

Conclusion: 在所选闭合框架下，轴向环振的主要可观测印记来自物质支撑分支本身的存在性，而非迹耦合$α$的直接变化；常数$(w_r,w_t)$模型应解读为有效各向异性应力而非微观物理流体。

Abstract: We study odd-parity gravitational perturbations of static black holes in trace-quadratic $f(R,T)=R+αT^2$ gravity supported by an anisotropic effective fluid with constant closure parameters $(w_r,w_t)$. From the unreduced axial system and its principal symbol, we identify the sector of parameter space that supports a regular horizon, asymptotic flatness, and hyperbolic odd-sector evolution. Within this closure the admissible branch lies at negative $w_r$, while the commonly used positive-$w_r$ family fails the background regularity test and is kept only as a numerical comparison branch. On static admissible backgrounds the odd sector is exactly equivalent to Einstein gravity coupled to a frozen effective anisotropic fluid, so the physical axial spectrum is governed by a single gauge-invariant master equation. For the anchored branch $(w_r,w_t)=(-0.2,0.15)$ we compute the fundamental axial $\ell=2$ quasinormal mode with an exact Chebyshev solve. The mass-normalized spectrum differs from Schwarzschild by about $22\%$, whereas no statistically resolved direct $α$-dependence appears within the conservative spectral envelope over $0\le α/M^2\le 0.3$. We also construct a coupled physics-informed neural network for the unreduced two-field eigenproblem and use it to benchmark the inadmissible comparison branch. A closure-level audit of the anchored family shows positive diagnostic combinations associated with the null, weak, and dominant energy conditions, denominator safety in the modified balance law, and an effective exterior mass fraction of about $20\%$, while indicating that the constant-$(w_r,w_t)$ model should be read as an effective anisotropic stress rather than as a microphysical fluid. Within this closure, the main observable imprint in axial ringdown comes from the existence of the matter-supported branch itself, not from direct variation of the trace coupling.

</details>


### [141] [GUP-corrected black holes: Thermodynamic properties, evaporation time and shadow constraint from EHT Observations of M87* and Sgr A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06548)
*H. Chen,S. -H. Dong,E. Maghsoodi,S. Hassanabadi,J. Křiž,S. Zare,H. Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将含线性和二次动量项的广义不确定性原理（GUP）应用于史瓦西黑洞度量，研究量子效应对黑洞热力学和蒸发过程的影响，并利用M87*和Sgr A*的EHT数据对GUP参数进行约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子引力效应（特别是GUP）对黑洞热力学和蒸发过程的影响，并通过观测数据对GUP参数进行约束。

Method: 在半经典框架下推导GUP修正的霍金温度；通过启发式分析计算熵、热容和亥姆霍兹自由能；利用图形方法分析黑洞蒸发过程及事件视界半径、光子球半径和阴影轮廓；结合EHT观测数据（M87*和Sgr A*）约束GUP参数α和β。

Result: 存在GUP效应时霍金温度存在最大值；GUP修正了热力学量并影响黑洞相变；Sgr A*比M87*提供更强的约束；β增大时α的约束范围扩大；较小的α值下阴影半径更接近Sgr A*的观测值。

Conclusion: GUP显著影响黑洞热力学和蒸发过程，Sgr A*的EHT数据对GUP参数提供了更严格的限制。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we implement the generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) with linear and quadratic moment for Schwarzschild black hole metric in order to study the influence of quantum effect on the thermodynamics and evaporation of black hole. To this end, we first derive the GUP-modified Hawking temperature of a black hole in the semi-classical framework. Due to the existence of the GUP effect, there is a maximum Hawking temperature. We determine the entropy, heat capacity and Helmholtz free energy with heuristic analysis that investigates the particle absorbed by black hole. Furthermore, we also verify that these quantities are modified by the GUP, the influence of quantum effect on the black hole phase transition is discussed in detail. Then, we analyze the black hole evaporation process in the mentioned framework and examine the obtained results by graphical methods and compare them with each other. We likewise explore the behavior of the event horizon radius, photon sphere radius, and shadow silhouette when influenced by the GUP-corrected Schwarzschild black hole (GCSBH) parameters. We intend to establish restrictions for $α$ by utilizing the event horizon telescope (EHT) data for M87* and Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*). Our findings show that Sgr A* provides more robust constraints. As the parameter $β$ grows, the range of constraints for $α$ expands. For Sgr A* one, we find that the shadow radius is close to the observed value at smaller values of $α$.

</details>


### [142] [Robustness of the relativistic intermediate-axis instability around dark-matter-dressed rotating black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06557)
*Mohsen Fathi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: DARK-FLIP II通过半解析框架检验了暗物质（DM）对旋转黑洞周围非轴对称物质元素翻转频率的影响，发现DM归一化增加会降低翻转频率，而更延展的DM剖面会削弱局部响应，从而验证了翻转频率可作为DM敏感的定向时钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验DARK-FLIP I中提出的相对论性中间轴不稳定性（IAI）框架对暗物质环境的鲁棒性，回答局部暗物质剖面变化如何影响翻转频率这一核心问题。

Method: 使用受控有效响应模型（ERM），通过改变暗物质归一化、剖面标度半径、中间主转动惯量、有效潮汐耦合、初始扰动和初始取向等参数，采用Einasto、cored-NFW和Hernquist暗物质模型，进行一维扫描、二维响应图、剖面对比图、时域翻转模拟、剖面时间响应诊断和局部投影发射率代理分析。

Result: 增加封闭暗物质归一化会降低相对于Kerr黑洞的翻转频率，而更延展的暗物质剖面会削弱局部响应。

Conclusion: DARK-FLIP II进一步强化了翻转频率作为受控的暗物质敏感定向时钟解释。

Abstract: DARK-FLIP I introduced a semi-analytical and Python-based framework for studying a relativistic version of the intermediate-axis instability (IAI) of a coherent non-axisymmetric matter element around rotating black holes dressed by dark matter (DM). In this second paper I test the robustness of that idea. The main question is simple: if the local environment is changed by the DM profile, how does the flip frequency respond? To answer this, I use a controlled effective response model (ERM), not a full accretion or radiative-transfer simulation. The flip frequency is therefore treated as a diagnostic orientation-modulation timescale, not as a direct quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) model. I vary the DM normalization, profile scale radius, intermediate principal moment of inertia, effective tidal coupling, initial perturbation, and initial orientation. Einasto and regularized cored Navarro--Frenk--White (cored-NFW) profiles are used as the main DM models, while Hernquist is kept as a control benchmark. The analysis includes one-dimensional scans, two-dimensional response maps, profile-contrast maps, time-domain flip simulations, a profile timing-response diagnostic, and a local projected-emissivity proxy. The results show a clear perturbative trend: increasing the enclosed DM normalization decreases the flip frequency relative to Kerr, while more extended profiles weaken the local response. DARK-FLIP II therefore strengthens the interpretation of the flip frequency as a controlled DM-sensitive orientation clock.

</details>


### [143] [Measuring a Black Hole's Area Immediately after Merger: A Direct-Wave Test of Hawking's Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06592)
*Adrian Ka-Wai Chung,Kelvin Ka-Ho Lam,Anna Liu,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种引力波方法，从合并前直接波中推断克尔等效黑洞视界面积，并将其应用于GW250114事件，结果与克尔残余黑洞一致，为霍金面积定律提供了新的近合并测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞面积是视界热力学背后的几何变量，但目前缺乏从直接波中测量面积的有效方法。

Method: 引入引力波方法，从近合并信号中的直接波（而非晚期准正则振铃）推断克尔等效视界面积，并拟合直接波的频率和阻尼率作为视界量。

Result: 应用于GW250114，在峰值振幅前3-4.5M的分析窗口内，得到的面积与克尔残余黑洞一致。

Conclusion: 这是首次利用直接波进行面积测量，为霍金面积定律提供了新的近合并检验手段。

Abstract: Black-hole area is the geometric variable behind horizon thermodynamics. We introduce a gravitational-wave method to infer a Kerr-equivalent horizon area from direct waves in the near-merger signal, before quasinormal ringing dominates at late times. Applied to GW250114, and interpreting the fitted direct-wave frequency and damping rate as horizon quantities, we find that analyses initiated $3$--$4.5M$ before the peak-amplitude time yield an area consistent with the Kerr remnant. This result gives a first area measurement using direct waves and a new near-merger test of Hawking's area law.

</details>


### [144] [Radial Oscillations of Viscous Stars at Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06600)
*Amanda Guerrieri,Gabriel S. Rocha,Gabriel S. Denicol,Raissa F. P. Mendes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Israel-Stewart和Navier-Stokes理论框架下，研究了包含热扩散和有限温度状态方程的相对论性恒星径向振荡谱，发现热模从纯阻尼过渡到传播行为，并在有限恒星几何中实现了相对论性第二声，且热模与流体振荡谱的耦合为温度参数的二阶小量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前相对论性恒星径向振荡分析扩展到包含热扩散和热力学自洽的有限温度状态方程，填补Israel-Stewart框架中最后一个耗散自由度的空白。

Method: 在Israel-Stewart和Navier-Stokes理论中引入热流和有限温度状态方程（冷多方球模型的Sommerfeld型微扰），通过分析离散恒星振荡谱与平直时空色散关系的关系，构建解析近似。

Result: 热模在超过临界泛音数时从纯阻尼转变为传播行为（有限尺度下的第二声实现）；恒星有限几何可将基模热模也推入传播区；热模与流体振荡谱的耦合为温度参数的二阶小量；离散谱可由平直时空色散关系解析构造。

Conclusion: 在Israel-Stewart框架中完成了粘性恒星径向振荡的完整分析，包含了最后一个耗散自由度（热扩散）。

Abstract: We study the radial oscillation spectrum of relativistic stars within Israel-Stewart and Navier-Stokes theories, extending previous analyses to include heat diffusion and a thermodynamically consistent finite-temperature equation of state. The inclusion of heat flux gives rise to a distinct thermal sector in the mode spectrum, whose structure closely mirrors the dispersion relations of an infinite dissipative fluid. Within Israel-Stewart theory, the thermal modes transition from purely damped to propagating behavior above a critical overtone number, providing a finite-size realization of relativistic second sound in compact stars. Remarkably, the finite stellar geometry can push even the fundamental thermal mode into the propagating regime -- a feature with no continuum analogue. For the class of equations of state considered here, where finite-temperature corrections enter as controlled, Sommerfeld-type perturbations of a cold polytrope, the thermal sector couples only weakly to the ordinary fluid oscillation spectrum, with the coupling being of second order in a suitable temperature parameter. We further show that the discrete stellar spectrum is well captured by an analytic ansatz constructed from the flat-spacetime dispersion relations, with the star's finite radius discretizing the continuous mode structure. Our results complete the analysis of radial oscillations of viscous stars by incorporating the last remaining dissipative degree of freedom within the Israel-Stewart framework.

</details>


### [145] [Gravitational waveforms from binaries in higher-derivative gravity: a Love story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07070)
*Pablo A. Cano,Francesco Fucito,Jose F. Morales,Alejandro Ruipérez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高阶导数引力理论（包含Riemann张量的三次和四次收缩项）中，测试粒子围绕非旋转黑洞的引力波辐射。推导了含源项的奇偶宇称扰动的控制方程，构建了Post-Minkowskian展开解。针对圆轨道双星系统，计算了波形的Post-Newtonian展开以及无穷远处的能量和角动量通量。发现高阶导数修正始终出现在5PN阶，且与ℓ=2模扰动的Love数成正比。分析结果通过数值计算验证，并扩展至更大速度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高阶导数引力理论（修正引力理论的一种）对引力波辐射的修正，以检验广义相对论在强场区域的适用性，并为未来引力波观测提供理论预言。

Method: 1. 推导了含任意源项的黑洞奇偶宇称扰动主方程；2. 构建了齐次主方程的Post-Minkowskian展开解；3. 针对圆轨道双星系统，计算波形及能量/角动量通量的Post-Newtonian展开；4. 通过数值计算验证解析结果。

Result: 高阶导数修正始终出现在5PN（后牛顿）阶，且修正幅度与描述ℓ=2模扰动下几何可变形性的Love数成正比。数值计算验证了解析结果，并将分析扩展至更大速度范围。

Conclusion: 高阶导数引力理论对引力波的修正具有普适的5PN阶特征，且与Love数成正比，这为未来引力波观测检验修正引力理论提供了明确的理论模板。

Abstract: We study the emission of gravitational waves by a test particle orbiting a non-rotating black hole in higher-derivative gravity theories with cubic and quartic contractions of the Riemann tensor. To this aim, we first derive the master equations describing even- and odd-parity perturbations in the presence of an arbitrary source term, and then construct a Post-Minkowskian expansion of the solutions to the homogeneous master equations. Specializing to a circular binary system, we compute the Post-Newtonian expansion of the waveform, as well as the energy and angular-momentum fluxes at infinity. We show that higher-derivative corrections to the waveform and to the fluxes always appear at 5PN order, and are universally proportional to the Love number describing the deformability of the geometry under the $\ell=2$ mode perturbation. These analytical results are validated against numerical computations, which also allow us to extend the analysis to larger velocities.

</details>


### [146] [Vanishing of all redshift modes in Schwarzschild ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07173)
*Adrien Kuntz,Matteo Della Rocca*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究粒子坠入黑洞产生的黑洞环振，发现红移模、视界模和直接波在可观测波形中振幅为零，并证明这种抵消源于因果律和准正则模式求和过程中的精确抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清黑洞环振中红移模等贡献是否能在可观测波形中出现，解决现有文献中关于这些模式物理意义的争议。

Method: 通过因果律分析源积分格林函数在光锥上的消失特性，证明红移模贡献的抵消；并论证准正则模式求和过程中各阶模式的精确抵消机制，指出脉冲项作为抵消项的作用。

Result: 所有红移模、视界模和直接波在可观测波形中振幅为零；准正则模式各阶模式虽携带红移模贡献，但求和后精确抵消；脉冲项是实施该抵消的逆项。

Conclusion: 红移模在可观测波形中不可观测，为准正则模式激发系数的正则化提供了物理动机。

Abstract: Several studies of black hole ringdown from particles plunging into black holes have identified contributions decaying at integer multiples of the surface gravity, called redshift modes, horizon modes, and direct waves. We show that, for Schwarzschild black holes, every one of these contributions has vanishing amplitude in the observable waveform. The cancellation follows from causality, which forces the source-integrated Green function to vanish on the light cone. Individual quasi-normal mode overtones still carry non-zero redshift-mode contributions, but these cancel exactly once the sum over overtones is performed; the so-called impulsive contribution to the waveform acts precisely as the counterterm enforcing this cancellation. Finally, we provide a motivation to the standard regularization of quasinormal mode excitation coefficients since divergences give rise to vanishing redshift modes.

</details>


### [147] [From (Hidden) Symmetries to Stealth Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07212)
*David Kubiznak,Robert B. Mann,Marek Milička*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了(共形)Killing-Yano张量可生成p-形式隐形解，将先前关于Killing向量与Proca理论的构造推广到任意背景时空中的高阶对称性，并给出了若干时空实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前工作中发现的(共形)Killing向量与特定bumblebee型Proca理论中隐形向量解的联系，推广到更一般的隐藏对称性——即(共形)Killing-Yano张量所对应的p-形式隐形解。

Method: 通过数学构造，证明(共形)Killing-Yano张量可在任意背景时空中生成对应的p-形式隐形解，沿袭了Killing向量情形的方法论。

Result: 成功构建了与(共形)Killing-Yano张量对应的p-形式隐形解，提供了多个不同时空背景下的具体例子。

Conclusion: 该构造适用于任意背景，为时空对称性提供了一种"物理可视化"手段，表明隐藏对称性可表现为高阶形式的隐形毛发。

Abstract: In a recent paper, Arxiv:2605.23077, we have demonstrated that (conformal) Killing vectors give rise to stealth vector solutions of a specific bumblebee-type Proca theory supplemented by fine tuned curvature terms. Here we show that such a construction readily generalizes to hidden symmetries encoded in (conformal) Killing-Yano tensors, giving rise to the corresponding p-form stealth solutions. Similar to what happens with Killing vectors, the construction works on any background, providing a "physical visualization" of its symmetries. Several examples of spacetimes with so constructed p-form stealth hair are presented.

</details>


### [148] [Loss of the Scaling Attractor in Self-Gravitating Domain Wall Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07296)
*Zhen-Min Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过将速度依赖单尺度模型与弗里德曼方程和辐射能量转移耦合，自洽地研究了畴壁网络的演化。分析表明，引力反作用会改变相空间结构：固定背景下的辐射纪标度解从稳定吸引子变为鞍点，且物理相空间中不存在稳定不动点。标度行为仅是瞬态过程，所有轨迹最终都进入壁主导且运动学受阻的状态，壁在共动坐标中冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究假设畴壁网络在固定辐射或物质主导背景上达到相对论标度 regime（无受挫猜想），但忽略了缺陷网络自身的引力反作用。本文旨在自洽地考虑引力反馈，探究畴壁网络的真实演化行为。

Method: 将速度依赖单尺度模型与弗里德曼方程及辐射能量转移方程耦合，构建自治动力系统。通过相空间分析研究固定点及其稳定性。

Result: 1) 引力反作用将辐射纪标度解从稳定吸引子变为鞍点；2) 物理相空间中不存在稳定固定点；3) 标度行为仅为瞬态过程；4) 所有轨迹最终趋向壁主导且运动学受阻（共动坐标中冻结）的状态。

Conclusion: 自引力畴壁网络中，标度吸引子不被保持，壁主导的晚期受挫动力学是普遍结果。受挫猜想在自洽引力系统中不成立。

Abstract: Domain-wall(DW) networks are known to approach a relativistic scaling regime on fixed radiation- and matter-dominated backgrounds, forming the basis of the no-frustration conjecture. However, this picture assumes that the defect network remains gravitationally subdominant. We investigate the self-consistent evolution of DWs by coupling the velocity-dependent one-scale model to the Friedmann equation and radiation energy transfer. The resulting autonomous system allows the cosmic expansion history to evolve dynamically rather than being imposed externally. We demonstrate analytically that gravitational backreaction qualitatively changes the phase-space structure: the radiation-era scaling solution, which is a stable attractor on a fixed background, becomes a saddle once the expansion rate is promoted to a dynamical degree of freedom. Furthermore, we establish that no stable fixed point exists within the physical phase space. Consequently, the scaling regime survives only as a transient stage, and all trajectories are driven toward a wall dominated and kinematically frustrated state in which the walls freeze in comoving coordinates. Our results demonstrate that the scaling attractor is not preserved in self-gravitating DW networks and reveal the generic late-time frustration dynamics of wall domination.

</details>


### [149] [Red noise and evolving signals: a complete frequentist approach to supermassive black hole binary searches with pulsar timing array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07330)
*Xuan Tao,Boris Goncharov,Yiqian Qian,Yan Wang,Soumya D. Mohanty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义似然比检验（GLRT）及相关$\mathcal{T}$-统计量，用于在脉冲星测时阵列数据中探测来自演化中的孤立超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHB）的引力波。该方法通过半解析最大化脉冲星相位参数似然，结合粒子群优化，有效解决了信号与噪声参数同时估计的高维难题，并在模拟中实现了高检测概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如$\mathcal{F}$-统计量）局限于非演化源，未同时考虑地球项和脉冲星项及未知的脉冲星距离，可能导致估计偏差。需要一种能够处理演化SMBHB信号且计算可行的检测统计量。

Method: 采用广义似然比检验（GLRT）框架，推导$\mathcal{T}$-统计量。通过半解析方式最大化脉冲星相位参数的似然，大幅降低拟合问题维度，随后利用粒子群优化（PSO）对剩余参数进行高效全局优化。

Result: 模拟表明，对于啁啾质量$\mathcal{M}=10^{9.2} M_\odot$、信噪比20的演化SMBHB信号，在30颗脉冲星、100 ns白噪声残差及脉冲星特定红噪声的阵列中，该统计量在虚警概率0.06下达到100%的检测概率。

Conclusion: 所提$\mathcal{T}$-统计量克服了传统频域方法的局限性，为在PTA数据中搜索演化SMBHB引力波信号提供了有效且无偏的检测手段。

Abstract: Searches for gravitational waves (GWs) from isolated supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs) in pulsar timing array (PTA) data require simultaneous estimation of signal and noise parameters, so the dimensionality of the fit scales with the number of observed pulsars. This computational difficulty is exacerbated when source evolution from GW emission is included, since retaining both Earth and pulsar terms introduces the unknown pulsar distances. Existing frequentist methods such as the $\mathcal{F}$-statistic, restricted so far to non-evolving sources, effectively, imply a circular analysis, which may lead to biased estimators. We present a Generalized Likelihood Ratio Test (GLRT) and the associated $\mathcal{T}$-statistic that overcomes the aforementioned limitations. The formulation of the GLRT extends earlier work in which the dimensionality of the fitting problem was drastically reduced by semi-analytical maximization of the likelihood over the pulsar phase parameters, followed by efficient global optimization over the remaining parameters using Particle Swarm Optimization. Our simulations demonstrate that for an evolving SMBHB signal with chirp mass $\mathcal{M}=10^{9.2}\,M_\odot$ and signal-to-noise ratio $20$, this detection statistic achieves a $100\%$ detection probability at a false-alarm probability of $0.06$ in a 30-pulsar timing array, which is characterized by a $100~\mathrm{ns}$ root-mean-square white noise residual and pulsar-specific red noise.

</details>


### [150] [Stochastic scalar-tensor inflation and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07467)
*Yoann Launay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种规范无关的粗粒化方法，将随机暴胀框架从广义相对论扩展到更广泛的标量-张量理论（包括Gauss-Bonnet、广义Brans-Dicke、Horndeski等），并讨论了多场暴胀等自然扩展，为早期宇宙的随机动力学提供了统一的形式体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机暴胀形式体系目前仅在广义相对论中得到了自洽表述，尚未推广到更一般的早期宇宙理论（如标量-张量理论）。本文旨在填补这一空白，为更广泛的非线性标量-张量理论建立随机框架。

Method: 作者将规范无关的粗粒化程序应用于有效场论（EFT of dark energy）的线性方程，通过识别EFT中对应的系数，将每种理论映射到其自身的随机运动方程，从而实现对广义相对论以外理论的扩展。

Result: 成功将随机暴胀框架推广至Gauss-Bonnet、广义Brans-Dicke、Horndeski和braiding理论等多个具体例子，其中大部分是此前未有的；还展示了全广义相对论中多场暴胀的粗粒化。

Conclusion: 该粗粒化程序具有通用性，不仅适用于上述理论，还有潜力应用于暴胀之外的其他宇宙学场景，为构建现象学上完整的随机框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: During cosmological inflation, inhomogeneities arising from quantum vacuum fluctuations are stretched to become super-Hubble and effectively classical. As many scenarios of the origin involve nonlinearities or a breakdown of perturbativity in the infrared, the limitations of quantum field theory can be addressed using a stochastic description of the dynamics, the so-called stochastic inflation paradigm. However, the stochastic formalism was only recently formulated consistently within full General Relativity and has not yet been extended to more general theories of the early universe, which is the subject of this work.
  In order to find the stochastic sources for a wide class of fully nonlinear scalar-tensor theories, we apply our gauge-agnostic coarse-graining procedure to the linear equations of the effective field theory of dark energy. Each theory can then be mapped to its own set of stochastic equations of motion by identifying the corresponding coefficients in the EFT. We illustrate this with a few concrete and, in most cases, unprecedented examples, including Gauss-Bonnet, generalized Brans-Dicke, Horndeski, and braiding theories.
  Finally, we discuss other natural extensions to provide a phenomenologically complete stochastic framework. For example, we showcase the coarse-graining of multifield inflation in full General Relativity and argue for the generality of our procedure and thus its potential applications beyond the realm of inflation.

</details>
